Detailed algorithm plan for final code:
1. Utility normalize, FFT conv, score.
2. Generate N candidates of various types and lengths? Choose final best.
   - Base: if height_sequence_1 exists length n0, resample? Use it as one start (normalized) for its length.
   - For chosen n (maybe same n0): generate smooth priors: random trigonometric, beta densities, two-block asymmetric, monotone power, linear, step, etc.
   - Optionally binary random/balanced starts.
3. For each candidate, run a local optimizer within allocated time. Keep best exact.

To maximize improvement, maybe allocate most time to polishing the best found from previous with advanced optimizer. If previous is good local, advanced optimizer may push below target. Use time left for other starts.

Adam on u may be more automatic. Let's compare.

- parameter u length n (log p). To keep sum, p=softmax(u). But p values around1/n, u≈-log n + small. Softmax stable. Initial u=log(p_initial)+constant. Objective W(u)=J(softmax(u)). Detailed `mirror_adam_search(p, L, m, n, time_limit, rng, verbose=False)`:
- p = normalize. L,m,n.
- deadline = now + time_limit.
- best_p=p.copy(); best_score=score_conv(p)
- beta = EMA params: m_g zeros, v_g zeros, t=0
- lr = 0.15
- beta1=0.9, beta2=0.99
- fail=0, stalls=0
- temp_type: start with top tolerance high (smooth) then decrease. Use `tol = 0.1` initially? Need units vmax=n*c. vmax around 0.75-1.5? Actually v=n*c, maxv = objective/2. Initial uniform maxv=1. For good ~0.76. Range c width maybe 0.25. Use tol based on maxv: start 0.12, end 0.0 or 0.01. Alternatively use softmax temp.
   Let's implement weights by top with tolerance and optional softmax fallback.
   Function `get_weights(c, stage)`:
      v = n*c; vmax=max(v); if vmax<=0 return uniform.
      tol = max(tol_min*maxv, tol_abs?) stage: tol = current_tol*maxv. current_tol schedule: start 0.15, slowly to 0.0 over 80% of time, min 0.0. But if too many active, gradient may be average over broad set, maybe good early. min_active maybe 32. active = v >= vmax - tol. If count > max_active (e.g., 4096), top max_active? If count < min_active, take top min_active.
      uniform over active.
   This exact subgradient may be better than softmax. But if tol=0, active count might be ~number of maxima (maybe one/two) causing noisy. Use min_active 64 to average near-max.
- Gradient compute function `grad_from_weights(w, p, L)`:
   W = np.fft.rfft(w, L)
   p_rev = p[::-1]
   Frev = np.fft.rfft(p_rev, L)
   g = 2.0 * np.fft.irfft(W * Frev, L)[n-1:2*n-1]
   g = np.maximum(g,0)
   return g
   But w length m; if active near max, many zeros; FFT length L. Good.
- At each iteration:
   if time left <0.2 break.
   c = conv_self(p); sc = 2*n*max(c)
   update best.
   w = weights
   g = grad; if maxg<=0: break
   d = g / maxg
   d -= mean(d)
   t+=1
   m_g = beta1*m_g + (1-beta1)*d
   v_g = beta2*v_g + (1-beta2)*d*d
   mhat=m_g/(1-beta1**t); vhat=v_g/(1-beta2**t)
   step = lr * mhat/(sqrt(vhat)+1e-8)
   # clip step to avoid under/overflow
   step = np.clip(step, -5.0, 5.0)
   p2 = p * np.exp(-step)
   p2 = clip positive, normalize (if sum fail)
   c2=conv_self(p2); sc2=2*n*max(c2)
   accept = sc2 < best_score - 1e-12 or sc2 < sc - 1e-10
   If accept: p=p2, update best if improves, fail=0, stalls=0, lr=min(0.8, lr*1.03) maybe; else: sc2 maybe used? If sc2 slightly worse, try smaller lr? We can line search: try factor 0.5,0.25 computing conv? To save, only if fail>0. def _normalize_positive(a, eps=0.0):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).copy()
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    if eps>0: a=np.maximum(a,eps)
    s=a.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=1e-300:
        a.fill(1.0); s=a.sum()
    a/=s
    return a
```

```
def _fft_self_conv(a,L,m):
    f=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(f*f,L)[:m]
    c=np.ascontiguousarray(c)
    np.maximum(c,0.0,out=c)
    # correct tiny sum error? not needed.
    return c
```
For normalized p, sum c =1 up to round. We may renormalize c? Score uses max; if FFT numerical small positive, okay. To avoid sum errors, maybe `c /= np.sum(c)`? Since p normalized, self conv sum should 1. But clipping negative may alter sum. We can renormalize c to sum1 to reduce numerical bias: `s=c.sum(); if s>0: c/=s`. This ensures score scaling. For exact score, if p sum1, true sum1. Good. But if p has negative clipped? We maintain positive. Do renormalize.

```
def _score_c(c,n):
    return float(2.0*n*np.max(c))
```

```
def _score_p(p,L,m,n):
    c=_fft_self_conv(p,L,m)
    return _score_c(c,n), c
```

Gradient:
```
def _grad_from_weights(w, p, L, n, m):
    W=np.fft.rfft(w,L)
    # p_rev FFT via relation maybe
    Fp=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
    # compute Frev: for rfft, frequency index k corresponds angle 2πk/L.
    # p_rev DFT = exp(-i2πk(n-1)/L) * conj(Fp[k])? Since p real and zero padded.
    # For rfft real arrays, conj(Fp) works and phase factor.
    k=np.arange(np.size(Fp))
    phase=np.exp(-1j*2.0*np.pi*k*(n-1)/float(L))
    Frev = np.conj(Fp) * phase
    g=2.0*np.fft.irfft(W*Frev,L)[n-1:m]
    g=np.ascontiguousarray(g)
    np.maximum(g,0.0,out=g)
    return g
```
Need ensure relation correct for rfft length L. Let's test with simple p=[a,b], n=2, L=3? rfft length3 freqs k=0,1. Fp[0]=a+b, Fp[1]=a + b e^{-i2π/3}. p_rev=[b,a]. Frev[0]=a+b. conj(Fp[0])*phase= a+b. For k=1: conj(Fp[1])=a + b e^{i2π/3}; phase=e^{-i2π(1)/3}; product=a e^{-i2π/3}+ b e^{i2π/3}*e^{-i2π/3}=a e^{-i2π/3}+b = b + a e^{-i2π/3}. Correct Frev[1]. Good. For k=L/2 if L even, phase=-1^{n-1}, conj ok. So use relation, avoids rfft reversed.
But if p normalized and real, Fp complex. Good.
Need `np.exp` allocation each iter maybe cost; we can precompute `phase_rev` in a context? But no closures; we can pass L,n and compute once in search and pass to function? Could make helper to get phase. To avoid large top-level? We can compute inside; size ~8193, okay. Precompute in search: `freq_phase=np.exp(-1j*2π*np.arange(L//2+1)*(n-1)/L)`. def _weights_from_c(c, n, tol_frac, min_active, max_active):
    v=n*c
    vmax=float(np.max(v))
    if vmax<=0: return np.ones(m)/m? but m param needed.
    tol=max(1e-12, tol_frac*vmax)
    active = v >= vmax - tol
    cnt=active.sum()
    if cnt < min_active:
        # top min_active
        idx=np.argpartition(v, -min_active)[-min_active:] # but if min_active>v size
        active=np.zeros_like(v); active[idx]=True
    elif cnt > max_active:
        idx=np.argpartition(v, -max_active)[-max_active:]
        active=np.zeros_like(v); active[idx]=True
    w=np.zeros_like(v); w[active]=1.0/active.sum()
    return w
```
`argpartition` on 16k each iter can be costly but okay? Maybe use softmax instead to avoid sorting. Top sorting each iteration O(m log m) maybe heavy. We can use threshold without sorting: if cnt<min_active, add next values by sorting only if needed. Since many active likely >min. If tol too small, active maybe few; then sort. We can implement with np.sort when needed. Or use softmax weights (FFT no sort) for smooth. Maybe use softmax weights primarily, with temperature schedule; no sort. Let's do softmax for speed.
```
def _softmax_weights(v, temp):
    vmax=np.max(v)
    z=temp*(v-vmax)
    z=np.clip(z,-700,0)
    e=np.exp(z)
    s=e.sum()
    if s<=0 or not finite: w=np.ones_like(v)/len(v)
    else w=e/s
    return w
```
`v` length m=2n-1; exp each iter okay. Temperature units: v = n*c ~ score/2. Initial max maybe1 for uniform; after maybe0.75. To get many active, temp small (e.g. 1); to exact, temp large (e.g. 50). This is heuristic.

Random prior generation:
```
def _random_prior(n, rng, strength=1.0):
    t=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
    x=2*t-1
    z=rng.normal(0,0.3)
    z += rng.normal(0,0.5)*x
    z += rng.normal(0,0.5)*(x*x - 1/3)
    kmax=int(rng.integers(2,7))
    for k in range(1,kmax+1):
       amp=rng.uniform(0.05,0.5)/sqrt(k)
       ph=rng.uniform(0,2pi)
       z += amp*np.cos(2π*k*t+ph)
    noise=rng.normal(0,0.1,n); smooth; z+=noise
    p=np.exp(z-max); normalize
```
Use for restarts.

`resample_seq`:
```
def _resample_seq(seq,n):
   a=np.asarray(seq,float).ravel(); a=np.maximum(a,0); s=a.sum(); if s<=0: return ones
   c=np.cumsum(a); c=c/c[-1]
   xs=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
   vals=np.interp(xs, c, xs? no)
```

Let's write `_local_optimize` carefully:
```
def _local_optimize(p, L, m, n, time_limit, rng):
    if time_limit <= 0: return _normalize_positive(p), _score_p(...)
    deadline = time.monotonic()+float(time_limit)
    p = _normalize_positive(p)
    c = _fft_self_conv(p,L,m); cur_score = _score_c(c,n)
    best_p = p.copy(); best_score = cur_score
    temp = 4.0; lr=0.08; fail=0; stall=0
    beta1=0.9; beta2=0.99; mexp=np.zeros(n); vexp=np.zeros(n); t=0
    it=0
    freq_phase precompute
    while now < deadline:
       c = _fft_self_conv(p,L,m); cur_score=...
       if cur_score < best_score: best...
       # weights
       v = n*c
       vmax = max(v)
       # schedule temp maybe based on it/time and fail
       if fail>20: temp=max(2.0,temp*0.7)
       elif cur_score<best_score? temp=min(150,temp*1.05)
       elif cvx: ...
       w = _softmax_weights(v, temp)
       g = _grad_from_weights(w,p,L,n,m,freq_phase)
       gmax = g.max()
       if gmax<=0: break
       d = g/gmax
       dm = d - d.mean()
       t +=1
       mexp = beta1*mexp + (1-beta1)*dm
       vexp = beta2*vexp + (1-beta2)*(dm*dm)
       mhat = mexp/(1-beta1**t)
       vhat = vexp/(1-beta2**t)
       step = lr * mhat/(np.sqrt(vhat)+1e-8)
       step = np.clip(step, -3.0, 3.0)
       # try full and half
       accepted=False
       for scale in (1.0,0.5):
          p2 = p * np.exp(-scale*step)
          p2 = np.maximum(p2,0); s=p2.sum(); if not finite or s<=0: break
          p2/=s
          c2=_fft_self_conv(p2,L,m); sc2=_score_c(c2,n)
          Fcur = np.dot(w,c) # could compute before loop
          F2 = np.dot(w,c2)
          if sc2 < cur_score - 1e-14 or (sc2 < best_score + 1e-9 and F2 < Fcur - 1e-12):
              p=p2; c=c2; cur_score=sc2; accepted=True
              if cur_score < best_score - 1e-13:
                 best_p=p.copy(); best_score=cur_score
                 fail=0; stall=0; lr=min(0.25, lr*1.05)
              else:
                 fail=0
              break
       if not accepted:
          fail +=1; lr=max(0.01, lr*0.7); temp=max(2.0,temp*0.8)
          if fail>50:
             # random mix
             prior=_random_prior(n,rng)
             p = _normalize_positive(0.7*best_p + 0.3*prior)
             c=_fft_self_conv(p,L,m); cur_score=...
             mexp.fill(0); vexp.fill(0); t=0; fail=0; lr=0.08; temp=6.0
       stall update? if best not improved for >500: p=best_p; mexp reset; temp=lower; lr=0.05
       it+=1
       if it % 100 ==0: temp=min(150, temp*1.1)
    # final maybe exact internal with best
    return best_p, best_score
```
Need compute Fcur once per iter (dot w,c O(n)) cheap. But if w softmax, dot. For acceptance heuristic, Fcur = ∑w c. It has units 1/n. We can compare. Use tolerance.

def _fourier_projection(p,L,m,n,time_limit,rng):
    deadline=...
    p=normalize; c=conv; best_score=score
    best_p=p.copy()
    # target_v = best_score/2 * 0.98? best_score/2 = n maxc. We'll work with c cap = score/(2n)
    rate = 0.01
    delta_scale = 1e-5
    it=0
    while now<deadline:
       c=conv(p); sc=score; if sc<best: update
       cap_c = sc/(2*n) * (1 - rate)  # want lower
       # maybe floor cap_c to average*1.1
       avg_c = 1.0/m
       cap_c = max(cap_c, avg_c*1.05)
       c_target = _project_cap(c, cap_c)
       P = np.fft.rfft(p,L)
       # factor = sqrt((c_target + delta)/(c + delta)); where c small use 1? To avoid blowup, blend factor to 1 where c small.
       delta = 1e-6 * max(1e-30, c.mean())
       ratio = (c_target + delta)/(c + delta)
       ratio = np.clip(ratio, 0.0, 4.0) # prevent huge
       factor = np.sqrt(ratio)
       # Maybe only adjust top half? factor near1 where c/c_target similar.
       P_new = P * factor
       p_new = np.fft.irfft(P_new,L)[:n]
       p_new = np.maximum(p_new,0)
       s=p_new.sum(); if s>0: p_new/=s else p_new=best_p
       for alpha in (0.5,0.2,1.0):
          p_try=_normalize_positive((1-alpha)*p + alpha*p_new)
          sc_try=score; if sc_try < best_score: accept best? If sc_try<cur_score? We can accept if improves best; else maybe if <cur.
       if no accept: rate *=0.5; if rate<1e-4 break? increase delta; random mix?
    return best
```
Project cap function top-level:
```
def _project_cap(c, cap):
   c=np.asarray(c).copy()
   sum0=c.sum()
   if sum0<=0: return c/len? 
   if cap*len(c) < sum0: cap=sum0/len(c)
   if c.max() <= cap: 
      c/=sum0; return c
   # iterative waterfill
   work=c.copy(); active = work < cap
   excess = np.sum(np.maximum(work-cap,0))
   for _ in range(50):
      cnt = active.sum()
      if cnt==0: break
      delta = excess/cnt
      # if adding delta to all active stays below cap, done
      if work[active].max() + delta <= cap:
         work[active] += delta
         break
      else:
          # some become cap; set threshold
          thresh = cap - delta
          new_cap_mask = active & (work <= thresh)
          # those above thresh within active will hit cap
          # add enough to new active to reach cap, excess remaining for new active below?
          # Simpler: set all active with work+delta>=cap to cap, update active to those still below cap, recompute.
          hit = active & (work + delta >= cap)
          excess_hit = np.sum(cap - work[hit])
          excess -= excess_hit
          work[hit] = cap
          active &= ~hit
   # ensure sum
   work = np.maximum(work,0)
   s=work.sum(); if s>0: work/=s
   return work
```
This projection may not be exact but okay.

The factor in Fourier using c_target based on c may reduce max. But c_target via cap may have sum1. If cap > current max, c_target=c, factor1 no change. We set cap current*(1-rate), so c_target differs where current >cap. It reduces high frequencies? factor <1 where c high, >1 where c low. This redistributes Fourier magnitude. Could work. Use blend alpha to keep stability. If it improves score, accept. If not, reduce rate.

Potential issue: p_new after ifft might have negative; projecting positive can drastically change. Blend with old. We evaluate p_try; if any improves best, update best and p to p_try? We can set p=p_try even if not best? Maybe if it improves current. Keep best.

If no improvement after many, break.

This algorithm is experimental; okay.

Let's detail `_generate_candidates` to avoid too many.

```
def _generate_candidates(n, rng, base):
    cands=[]
    t=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/float(n)
    x=2*t-1
    # base
    if base is not None:
       b=np.asarray(base,float).ravel()
       if b.size==n: arr=b
       elif b.size>1: arr=_resample_seq(b,n)
       else: arr=np.ones(n)
       cands.append(_normalize_positive(arr))
    # linear
    for c in (-0.9,-0.6,-0.3,-0.1,0.0,0.1,0.3,0.6,0.9):
       q=1.0 + c*x; if max>0: cands.append(_normalize_positive(q, 1e-14))
    # powers
    for a in (0.3,0.5,0.8,1.2,2.0,4.0,8.0):
       cands.append(_normalize_positive((t+1e-6)**a))
       cands.append(_normalize_positive((1.0-t+1e-6)**a))
    # beta
    for a,beta in [(0.5,0.5),(0.5,1),(0.5,2),(1,1),(1,2),(2,1),(0.7,0.7),(0.7,1.5),(1.5,0.7),(3,1),(1,3)]:
       q=(t+1e-6)**(a-1) * (1.0-t+1e-6)**(beta-1)
       if q max>0: cands append
    # two blocks
    widths=(0.25,0.4,0.55,0.7,0.85,1.0)
    alphas=(0.1,0.2,0.3,0.5,0.7,0.85)
    for a in widths:
      for b in widths:
        for alpha in alphas:
           q=np.zeros(n)
           left = t < a
           q[left] += alpha / max(a,1e-6)
           right = t >= (1.0-b)
           q[right] += (1.0-alpha)/max(b,1e-6)
           if q.max()>0: cands.append(_normalize_positive(q))
    # random Fourier
    for _ in range(12): cands.append(_random_prior(n,rng))
    # binary random
    for alpha in (0.55,0.62,0.667,0.7,0.75):
       d=int(alpha*n); if d<1 continue
       idx=rng.choice(n,size=d,replace=False)
       p=np.zeros(n); p[idx]=1.0
       p += 1e-7
       cands.append(_normalize_positive(p))
    # If too many? maybe return cands
    return cands
```
Two blocks with intervals overlapping: q values high, normalize. Could produce all similar. 6*6*6=216. Good.

`_add_candidate`:
```
def _add_candidate(arrays, scores, arr, score, max_len=32):
    arr=np.asarray(arr,float)
    score=float(score)
    if not np.isfinite(score): return
    if arr.size==0: return
    if len(arrays)<max_len:
       arrays.append(arr.copy()); scores.append(score)
    else:
       worst=max(scores)
       if score < worst:
          idx=scores.index(worst)
          arrays[idx]=arr.copy(); scores[idx]=score

def _fourier_projection(p,L,m,n,time_limit,rng):

    c=_fft_self_conv(p,L,m); best_score=_score_c(c,n); best_p=p.copy()
    rate=0.02
    alpha_blend=0.5
    stall=0
    for _ in range(100000):
       if time.time... break
       c=_fft_self_conv(p,L,m); sc=_score_c(c,n)
       if sc < best_score - 1e-13: best...stall=0
       else: stall+=1
       cap_c = sc/(2.0*n) * (1.0 - rate)
       avg_c = 1.0/m
       cap_c = max(cap_c, avg_c*1.02)
       if cap_c >= c.max():
          # increase cap? if cannot lower, skip
          rate*=0.5
          continue
       ct=_project_cap(c, cap_c)
       P=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
       delta=1e-8*max(c.mean(),1e-30)
       ratio=(ct+delta)/(c+delta)
       ratio=np.clip(ratio, 0.05, 20.0)
       factor=np.sqrt(ratio)
       Pn=P*factor
       pnew=np.fft.irfft(Pn,L)[:n]
       pnew=np.maximum(pnew,0)
       s=pnew.sum()
       if not finite or s<=0: pnew=best_p
       else: pnew/=s
       accepted=False
       for alpha in (alpha_blend, 0.2, 1.0):
           ptr=_normalize_positive((1-alpha)*p + alpha*pnew, 1e-12)
           cpt=_fft_self_conv(ptr,L,m); sct=_score_c(cpt,n)
           if sct < best_score - 1e-13 or (sct < sc - 1e-12):
               p=ptr; c=cpt; sc=sct; accepted=True
               if sct < best_score - 1e-13: best...
               break
       if accepted:
          rate=min(rate*1.2,0.2)
       else:
          stall+=1; rate*=0.7; alpha_blend=min(alpha_blend*1.2,1.0)
          if stall>80 or rate<1e-4:
             prior=_random_prior(n,rng)
             p=_normalize_positive(0.8*best_p + 0.2*prior,1e-12)
             c=_fft_self_conv(p,L,m); sc=_score_c(c,n)
             stall=0; rate=0.02
    return best_p,best_score

`_project_cap`: Need avoid `active` changes causing infinite. Simpler implement waterfill robust:
```
def _project_cap(c, cap):
   c=np.array(c,float).copy(); s=c.sum()
   if s<=0: return c
   if cap*c.size < s: cap=s/c.size
   if c.max() <= cap:
      return c/s
   work=c.copy(); excess=float(np.sum(np.maximum(work-cap,0)))
   free = work < cap
   # We'll repeatedly add to free set.
   for _ in range(100):
      if not np.any(free): break
      cnt=int(np.sum(free))
      delta=excess/cnt
      if np.max(work[free]) + delta <= cap:
         work[free] += delta
         break
      # increase free values by delta; those exceeding cap set to cap
      work[free] += delta
      over = free & (work >= cap)
      # recover overshoot
      over_amount = float(np.sum(work[over] - cap))
      work[over] = cap
      excess += over_amount
      free = (work < cap)
   work = np.maximum(work,0)
   s2=work.sum()
   if s2>0: work/=s2
   return work
```
This is equivalent to projecting? It adds excess to all free, if some exceed, overshoot re-distributed. Should converge.

```
def _mws_direction(f, L, m, n, prior, inner, eta_start=2.0, eta_end=40.0):
    f=_normalize_positive(f); prior=_normalize_positive(prior)
    F=np.fft.rfft(f,L); Frev=np.fft.rfft(f[::-1],L)
    x=prior.copy(); y=np.full(m,1/m)
    avg=np.zeros_like(x); count=0; logprior=np.log(np.maximum(prior,1e-300))
    for it in range(inner):
       frac=0 if inner<=1 else it/(inner-1)
       eta=eta_start+(eta_end-eta_start)*frac
       X=np.fft.rfft(x,L)
       u=np.fft.irfft(F*X,L)[:m]; max(u,0)
       vu=n*u; vma=max; z=eta*(vu-vma); clip -50; y=exp(z); normalize
       avg += x; count+=1
       Y=np.fft.rfft(y,L)
       s=np.fft.irfft(Y*Frev,L)[(n-1):m]; max(s,0)
       ps=n*s; sma=max; zx=-eta*(ps-sma)+0.8*logprior - max; clip; x=exp(zx); normalize
    return _normalize(0.5*avg/count+0.5*x)
```
This computes a direction x. Then mix with f. We can call with random prior. The line search: try λ in [0.1,0.2,0.4,0.6,1] and smooth. Evaluate internal. If best < current, accept. This may improve.

def _mws_polish(p,L,m,n,time_limit,rng):
   deadline; best_p=p; best_score=score
   inner=20
   it=0
   while time:
      prior=_random_prior(n,rng)
      x=_mws_direction(best_p,L,m,n,prior,inner)
      # line search mix with x and smoothed x
      directions=[x, _smooth_vector(x)]
      for d in directions:
         for lam in (0.1,0.2,0.35,0.5,0.75,1.0):
            h=_normalize_positive((1-lam)*best_p + lam*d)
            sc,_=_score_p(h)
            if sc<best_score: best_p=h; best_score=sc
      # maybe use p=best for next
   return best
```

```
def _mws_polish(p,L,m,n,time_limit,rng):
   start=...; deadline=...
   p=_normalize_positive(p); c=_fft_self_conv(p); best_score=...; best_p=p.copy()
   inner=14
   no_imp=0
   while now < deadline-0.2:
      # time for one iteration estimate
      prior=_random_prior(n,rng)
      x=_mws_direction(best_p,L,m,n,prior,inner)
      # directions
      xs=[x]
      if best_p.size>9: xs.append(_smooth_vector(x,7))
      improved=False
      for d in xs:
         for lam in (0.05,0.1,0.2,0.35,0.5,0.7,1.0):
            h=_normalize_positive((1-lam)*best_p + lam*d)
            sc,_ = _score_p(h,L,m,n)
            if sc < best_score - 1e-13:
               best_p=h; best_score=sc; improved=True
               break
         if improved: break
      if improved:
         no_imp=0
         inner=min(inner+1,22)
      else:
         no_imp+=1
         if no_imp>50:
            # random restart from best mix
            h=_normalize_positive(0.8*best_p + 0.2*prior)
            sc,_=_score_p(h)
            if sc<best_score: best_p=h
            no_imp=0; inner=10
   return best_p,best_score
```

Let's code each function in detail.

`_normalize_positive`:
```
def _normalize_positive(a, eps=0.0):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.zeros(1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    if eps > 0.0:
        a = np.maximum(a, float(eps))
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        a.fill(1.0)
        s = float(a.sum())
    a /= s
    return a
```
If eps>0 and a had zeros, all at least eps. For n large, sum fine.

`_fft_self_conv`:
```
def _fft_self_conv(a, L, m):
    f = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(f * f, L)[:m]
    c = np.ascontiguousarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
    s = float(c.sum())
    if s > 0.0 and np.isfinite(s):
        c /= s
    else:
        c.fill(0.0)
        if c.size:
            c[0] = 1.0
    return c
```
Renormalizing c to sum1 may alter exact max if p sum1; good. But if p not normalized? We always normalize. In MWS direction x maybe normalized. Good.

`_score_c`:
```
def _score_c(c,n):
    mx=float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    return float(2.0*n*mx)
```

`_score_p`:
```
def _score_p(p,L,m,n):
    c=_fft_self_conv(p,L,m)
    return _score_c(c,n), c
```

`_grad_from_weights`:
```
def _grad_from_weights(w, p, L, n, m, phase):
    W = np.fft.rfft(w, L)
    Fp = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    Frev = np.conj(Fp) * phase
    g = 2.0 * np.fft.irfft(W * Frev, L)[n-1:m]
    g = np.ascontiguousarray(g, dtype=np.float64)
    np.maximum(g, 0.0, out=g)
    return g
```
No need Fp if using rfft p_rev? okay.

`_softmax_weights`:
```
def _softmax_weights(v, temp):
    v=np.asarray(v,float)
    m=v.size
    if m==0: return v
    vmax=float(np.max(v))
    if temp <= 1e-12 or vmax <= 0:
        return np.full(m, 1.0/m, dtype=np.float64)
    z = temp * (v - vmax)
    z = np.clip(z, -700.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0:
        return np.full(m,1.0/m)
    w=e/s
    return w
```

`_random_prior`:
```
def _random_prior(n, rng, strength=1.0):
    t=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/float(n)
    x=2.0*t-1.0
    z = strength*0.35*rng.normal()
    z += strength*0.55*rng.normal()*x
    z += strength*0.55*rng.normal()*(x*x - 1.0/3.0)
    kmax=int(rng.integers(2,7))
    for k in range(1,kmax+1):
        amp=strength*rng.uniform(0.05,0.5)/math.sqrt(float(k))
        ph=rng.uniform(0.0,2.0*math.pi)
        z += amp*np.cos((2.0*math.pi*k)*t + ph)
    noise = strength*0.12*rng.normal(0.0,1.0,n)
    if n > 5:
       noise = np.convolve(noise, np.ones(5)/5.0, mode='same')
    z += noise
    p=np.exp(z - float(np.max(z)))
    p=np.maximum(p,1e-300)
    return _normalize_positive(p)
```
But `z = strength*0.35*rng.normal()` gives scalar; adding arrays okay. Good.

`_resample_seq`:
```
def _resample_seq(seq, n):
    a=np.asarray(seq,float).ravel()
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0.0); a=np.maximum(a,0)
    s=a.sum()
    if a.size==0 or s<=0:
       return _normalize_positive(np.ones(n))
    m=a.size
    t_old=(np.arange(m)+0.5)/float(m)
    f=m*a
    t_new=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/float(n)
    f_new=np.interp(t_new, t_old, f)
    f_new=np.maximum(f_new,0)
    return _normalize_positive(f_new)
```

`_smooth_vector`:
```
def _smooth_vector(v, w=7):
    v=np.asarray(v,float).copy()
    if v.size <= int(w): return _normalize_positive(v)
    k=np.ones(int(w), dtype=np.float64)/float(w)
    y=np.convolve(v,k,mode='same')
    return _normalize_positive(y)
```

       if b.size == n:
           arr=b
       elif b.size > 1:
           arr=_resample_seq(b,n)
       else:
           arr=np.ones(n)
       cands.append(_normalize_positive(arr,1e-12))
    # linear
   ...
```
`_normalize_positive(arr,1e-12)` sets floor; floor 1e-12 times n =1e-8 total, negligible.

For two blocks, if a+b? no issue. `left = t < a`, `right = t >= (1.0-b)`. For b=1, right all; left overlap; q sum mass approx α+β? normalize. Good.

Beta: `(1.0 - t + 1e-6)` handles t=1? t max (n-0.5)/n <1, okay.

`_local_optimize`: implement with c maintained. Let's code.

```
def _local_optimize(p, L, m, n, time_limit, rng):
    p=_normalize_positive(p,1e-12)
    c=_fft_self_conv(p,L,m)
    cur_score=_score_c(c,n)
    best_p=p.copy(); best_score=cur_score
    if time_limit <= 0: return best_p,best_score
    start=time.monotonic(); deadline=start+float(time_limit)
    k=np.arange(L//2+1)
    phase=np.exp(-1j*2.0*math.pi*k*(n-1)/float(L))
    lr=0.15; temp=3.0; fail=0; stall=0; t=0
    mexp=np.zeros(n); vexp=np.zeros(n)
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 0.2:
        # temperature schedule
        prog = min(1.0, (time.monotonic()-start)/max(float(time_limit),1e-6))
        temp_sched = 3.0 + 80.0*prog*prog
        if temp < temp_sched:
            temp = temp_sched
        temp = min(220.0, temp)
        v = n*c
        w = _softmax_weights(v, temp)
        g = _grad_from_weights(w,p,L,n,m,phase)
        gmax=float(np.max(g))
        if not np.isfinite(gmax) or gmax <= 1e-300:
            break
        d = g/gmax
        dm = d - float(np.mean(d))
        t += 1
        mexp = 0.9*mexp + 0.1*dm
        vexp = 0.99*vexp + 0.01*(dm*dm)
        b1=0.9; b2=0.99
        mhat=mexp/(1.0 - b1**t)
        vhat=vexp/(1.0 - b2**t)
        step = lr * mhat/(np.sqrt(vhat)+1e-8)
        step=np.clip(step,-4.0,4.0)
        Fcur=float(np.dot(w,c))
        accepted=False
        for scale in (1.0,0.5):
            p2 = p * np.exp(-scale*step)
            p2 = np.maximum(p2,0.0)
            s2=float(p2.sum())
            if not np.isfinite(s2) or s2 <= 0:
                continue
            p2 /= s2
            c2=_fft_self_conv(p2,L,m)
            sc2=_score_c(c2,n)
            F2=float(np.dot(w,c2))
            if sc2 < cur_score - 1e-13 or (sc2 < best_score + 1e-9 and F2 < Fcur - 1e-13):
                p=p2; c=c2; cur_score=sc2; accepted=True
                if sc2 < best_score - 1e-13:
                    best_p=p.copy(); best_score=sc2; stall=0
                    lr=min(0.5, lr*1.05)
                break
        if accepted:
            fail=0
        else:
            fail += 1
            lr=max(0.02, lr*0.75)
            temp=max(2.0, temp*0.8)
            if fail > 40:
                prior=_random_prior(n,rng)
                mix=0.2+0.4*float(rng.random())
                p=_normalize_positive((1.0-mix)*best_p + mix*prior, 1e-12)
                c=_fft_self_conv(p,L,m); cur_score=_score_c(c,n)
                mexp.fill(0.0); vexp.fill(0.0); t=0
                fail=0; lr=0.15; temp=5.0
        # stall based on best not improved
        # We only increment stall when cur_score close? simpler: if cur_score >= best_score: stall +=1 else stall=0
        if cur_score >= best_score - 1e-13:
            stall += 1
        else:
            stall = 0
        if stall > 700:
            p=best_p.copy()
            c=_fft_self_conv(p,L,m); cur_score=_score_c(c,n)
            mexp.fill(0.0); vexp.fill(0.0); t=0
            lr=0.08; temp=4.0; fail=0; stall=0
            # small random kick
            prior=_random_prior(n,rng)
            p=_normalize_positive(0.95*best_p + 0.05*prior, 1e-12)
            c=_fft_self_conv(p,L,m); cur_score=_score_c(c,n)
    # final maybe sparsify? not here
    return best_p,best_score
```

`_mws_direction` code:
```
def _mws_direction(f,L,m,n,prior,inner,eta_start=2.0,eta_end=40.0):
    f=_normalize_positive(f); prior=_normalize_positive(prior)
    F=np.fft.rfft(f,L)
    Frev=np.fft.rfft(f[::-1],L)
    x=prior.copy()
    y=np.full(m,1.0/float(m),dtype=np.float64)
    avg=np.zeros_like(x)
    count=0
    logprior=np.log(np.maximum(prior,1e-300))
    for it in range(int(inner)):
        frac=0.0 if inner<=1 else float(it)/float(inner-1)
        eta=float(eta_start)+(float(eta_end)-float(eta_start))*frac
        X=np.fft.rfft(x,L)
        u=np.fft.irfft(F*X,L)[:m]
        u=np.ascontiguousarray(u); np.maximum(u,0,out=u)
        vu=n*u
        vma=float(np.max(vu))
        z=eta*(vu-vma); z=np.clip(z,-50,0)
        e=np.exp(z); s=float(e.sum())
        if finite: y=e/s else y.fill(1/m)
        avg += x; count +=1
        Y=np.fft.rfft(y,L)
        sseq=np.fft.irfft(Y*Frev,L)[(n-1):m]
        sseq=np.ascontiguousarray(sseq); np.maximum(sseq,0,out=sseq)
        ps=n*sseq; sma=float(np.max(ps))
        zx=-eta*(ps-sma)+0.8*logprior
        zm=float(np.max(zx)); zx=zx-zm; zx=np.clip(zx,-50,0)
        e=np.exp(zx); s=float(e.sum())
        if finite: x=e/s else x=prior.copy()
    if count==0: return x
    return _normalize_positive(0.5*(avg/count)+0.5*x)
```
Use variable `s` for sum and `sseq` not conflict. Good.

```python
"""
Search for non-negative step heights minimizing
    2*n*max(convolve(a, a)) / (sum(a)**2).

The algorithm combines:
  - a large library of analytic, random, and resampled starting points,
  - mirror/adaptive multiplicative gradient descent using FFT-based
    self-convolution and active-set weighting,
  - a multiplicative-weights minimax direction polish,
  - a Fourier-domain projection heuristic toward a capped convolution,
  - final sparsification and exact validation via evaluate_sequence.
"""

import math
import time
import numpy as np


def _normalize_positive(a, eps=0.0):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.zeros(1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    if eps > 0.0:
        a = np.maximum(a, float(eps))
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        a.fill(1.0)
        s = float(a.sum())
    a /= s
    return a


def _fft_self_conv(a, L, m):
    f = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(f * f, L)[:m]
    c = np.ascontiguousarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
    s = float(c.sum())
    if s > 0.0 and np.isfinite(s):
        c /= s
    else:
        c.fill(0.0)
        if c.size:
            c[0] = 1.0
    return c


def _score_c(c, n):
    mx = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    return float(2.0 * int(n) * mx)


def _score_p(p, L, m, n):
    c = _fft_self_conv(p, L, m)
    return _score_c(c, n), c


def _grad_from_weights(w, p, L, n, m, phase):
    W = np.fft.rfft(w, L)
    Fp = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    Frev = np.conj(Fp) * phase
    g = 2.0 * np.fft.irfft(W * Frev, L)[(n - 1):m]
    g = np.ascontiguousarray(g, dtype=np.float64)
    np.maximum(g, 0.0, out=g)
    return g


def _softmax_weights(v, temp):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    m = int(v.size)
    if m == 0:
        return v
    vmax = float(np.max(v))
    if temp <= 1e-12 or vmax <= 0.0:
        return np.full(m, 1.0 / float(m), dtype=np.float64)
    z = temp * (v - vmax)
    z = np.clip(z, -700.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(m, 1.0 / float(m), dtype=np.float64)
    return e / s


def _random_prior(n, rng, strength=1.0):
    t = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) / float(n)
    x = 2.0 * t - 1.0
    z = strength * 0.35 * float(rng.normal())
    z += strength * 0.55 * float(rng.normal()) * x
    z += strength * 0.55 * float(rng.normal()) * (x * x - 1.0 / 3.0)

    kmax = int(rng.integers(2, 7))
    for k in range(1, kmax + 1):
        amp = strength * float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.50)) / math.sqrt(float(k))
        ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi))
        z += amp * np.cos((2.0 * math.pi * k) * t + ph)

    noise = strength * 0.12 * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n)
    if n > 5:
        noise = np.convolve(noise, np.ones(5, dtype=np.float64) / 5.0, mode="same")
    z += noise

    p = np.exp(z - float(np.max(z)))
    p = np.maximum(p, 1e-300)
    return _normalize_positive(p)


def _resample_seq(seq, n):
    a = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if a.size == 0 or s <= 0.0:
        return _normalize_positive(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))
    m = int(a.size)
    t_old = (np.arange(m) + 0.5) / float(m)
    f = m * a
    t_new = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) / float(n)
    f_new = np.interp(t_new, t_old, f)
    f_new = np.maximum(f_new, 0.0)
    return _normalize_positive(f_new)


def _smooth_vector(v, w=7):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    w = int(w)
    if v.size <= w:
        return _normalize_positive(v)
    k = np.ones(w, dtype=np.float64) / float(w)
    y = np.convolve(v, k, mode="same")
    return _normalize_positive(y)


def _generate_candidates(n, rng, base):
    cands = []
    t = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) / float(n)
    x = 2.0 * t - 1.0

    if base is not None:
        b = np.asarray(base, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if b.size == n:
            arr = b
        elif b.size > 1:
            arr = _resample_seq(b, n)
        else:
            arr = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        cands.append(_normalize_positive(arr, 1e-12))

    for c in (-0.9, -0.6, -0.3, -0.1, 0.0, 0.1, 0.3, 0.6, 0.9):
        q = 1.0 + c * x
        if float(np.max(q)) > 0.0:
            cands.append(_normalize_positive(q, 1e-12))

    for a in (0.3, 0.5, 0.8, 1.2, 2.0, 4.0, 8.0):
        cands.append(_normalize_positive((t + 1e-6) ** a, 1e-12))
        cands.append(_normalize_positive((1.0 - t + 1e-6) ** a, 1e-12))

    beta_pairs = [
        (0.5, 0.5), (0.5, 1.0), (0.5, 2.0),
        (1.0, 1.0), (1.0, 2.0), (2.0, 1.0),
        (0.7, 0.7), (0.7, 1.5), (1.5, 0.7),
        (3.0, 1.0), (1.0, 3.0)
    ]
    for a, b2 in beta_pairs:
        q = (t + 1e-6) ** (a - 1.0) * (1.0 - t + 1e-6) ** (b2 - 1.0)
        if float(np.max(q)) > 0.0:
            cands.append(_normalize_positive(q, 1e-12))

    widths = (0.25, 0.40, 0.55, 0.70, 0.85, 1.00)
    alphas = (0.10, 0.20, 0.30, 0.50, 0.70, 0.85)
    for a in widths:
        for b2 in widths:
            for alpha in alphas:
                q = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                left = t < a
                right = t >= (1.0 - b2)
                q[left] += alpha / max(float(a), 1e-6)
                q[right] += (1.0 - alpha) / max(float(b2), 1e-6)
                if float(np.max(q)) > 0.0:
                    cands.append(_normalize_positive(q, 1e-12))

    for _ in range(14):
        cands.append(_random_prior(n, rng))

    for alpha in (0.55, 0.62, 0.667, 0.70, 0.75):
        d = max(1, int(round(alpha * float(n))))
        idx = rng.choice(n, size=d, replace=False)
        p = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        p[idx] = 1.0
        p += 1e-7
        cands.append(_normalize_positive(p))

    return cands


def _add_candidate(arrays, scores, arr, score, max_len=32):
    try:
        score = float(score)
    except Exception:
        return
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return
    arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64)
    if arr.size == 0:
        return
    if len(arrays) < int(max_len):
        arrays.append(arr.copy())
        scores.append(score)
    else:
        worst = max(scores)
        if score < worst:
            idx = scores.index(worst)
            arrays[idx] = arr.copy()
            scores[idx] = score


def _local_optimize(p, L, m, n, time_limit, rng):
    p = _normalize_positive(p, 1e-12)
    c = _fft_self_conv(p, L, m)
    cur_score = _score_c(c, n)
    best_p = p.copy()
    best_score = cur_score

    if time_limit <= 0.0:
        return best_p, best_score

    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + float(time_limit)

    kk = np.arange(L // 2 + 1)
    phase = np.exp(-1j * 2.0 * math.pi * kk * (n - 1) / float(L))

    lr = 0.15
    temp = 4.0
    fail = 0
    stall = 0
    bias1 = 0.0
    bias2 = 0.0
    mexp = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    vexp = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 0.2:
        prog = min(1.0, (time.monotonic() - start) / max(float(time_limit), 1e-6))
        temp_sched = 3.0 + 80.0 * prog * prog
        if temp < temp_sched:
            temp = temp_sched
        temp = min(220.0, temp)

        v = float(n) * c
        w = _softmax_weights(v, temp)
        g = _grad_from_weights(w, p, L, n, m, phase)
        gmax = float(np.max(g))
        if not np.isfinite(gmax) or gmax <= 1e-300:
            break

        d = g / gmax
        dm = d - float(np.mean(d))

        mexp = 0.9 * mexp + 0.1 * dm
        vexp = 0.99 * vexp + 0.01 * (dm * dm)
        bias1 = 0.1 + 0.9 * bias1
        bias2 = 0.01 + 0.99 * bias2
        mhat = mexp / max(bias1, 1e-12)
        vhat = vexp / max(bias2, 1e-12)
        step = lr * mhat / (np.sqrt(vhat) + 1e-8)
        step = np.clip(step, -4.0, 4.0)

        Fcur = float(np.dot(w, c))
        accepted = False

        for scale in (1.0, 0.5):
            p2 = p * np.exp(-scale * step)
            p2 = np.maximum(p2, 0.0)
            s2 = float(p2.sum())
            if not np.isfinite(s2) or s2 <= 0.0:
                continue
            p2 /= s2
            c2 = _fft_self_conv(p2, L, m)
            sc2 = _score_c(c2, n)
            F2 = float(np.dot(w, c2))

            if sc2 < cur_score - 1e-13 or (sc2 < best_score + 1e-9 and F2 < Fcur - 1e-13):
                p = p2
                c = c2
                cur_score = sc2
                accepted = True
                if sc2 < best_score - 1e-13:
                    best_p = p.copy()
                    best_score = sc2
                    stall = 0
                    lr = min(0.50, lr * 1.05)
                break

        if accepted:
            fail = 0
        else:
            fail += 1
            lr = max(0.02, lr * 0.75)
            temp = max(2.0, temp * 0.8)

            if fail > 40:
                prior = _random_prior(n, rng)
                mix = 0.2 + 0.4 * float(rng.random())
                p = _normalize_positive((1.0 - mix) * best_p + mix * prior, 1e-12)
                c = _fft_self_conv(p, L, m)
                cur_score = _score_c(c, n)
                mexp.fill(0.0)
                vexp.fill(0.0)
                bias1 = 0.0
                bias2 = 0.0
                fail = 0
                lr = 0.15
                temp = 5.0

        if cur_score >= best_score - 1e-13:
            stall += 1
        else:
            stall = 0

        if stall > 700:
            p = best_p.copy()
            c = _fft_self_conv(p, L, m)
            cur_score = _score_c(c, n)
            mexp.fill(0.0)
            vexp.fill(0.0)
            bias1 = 0.0
            bias2 = 0.0
            lr = 0.08
            temp = 4.0
            fail = 0
            stall = 0
            prior = _random_prior(n, rng)
            p = _normalize_positive(0.95 * best_p + 0.05 * prior, 1e-12)
            c = _fft_self_conv(p, L, m)
            cur_score = _score_c(c, n)

    return best_p, best_score


def _mws_direction(f, L, m, n, prior, inner, eta_start=2.0, eta_end=40.0):
    f = _normalize_positive(f)
    prior = _normalize_positive(prior)
    F = np.fft.rfft(f, L)
    Frev = np.fft.rfft(f[::-1], L)

    x = prior.copy()
    y = np.full(m, 1.0 / float(m), dtype=np.float64)
    avg = np.zeros_like(x)
    count = 0
    logprior = np.log(np.maximum(prior, 1e-300))

    for it in range(int(inner)):
        frac = 0.0 if int(inner) <= 1 else float(it) / float(int(inner) - 1)
        eta = float(eta_start) + (float(eta_end) - float(eta_start)) * frac

        X = np.fft.rfft(x, L)
        u = np.fft.irfft(F * X, L)[:m]
        u = np.ascontiguousarray(u, dtype=np.float64)
        np.maximum(u, 0.0, out=u)

        vu = float(n) * u
        vma = float(np.max(vu))
        z = eta * (vu - vma)
        z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 0.0)
        e = np.exp(z)
        s = float(e.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
            y.fill(1.0 / float(m))
        else:
            y = e / s

        avg += x
        count += 1

        Y = np.fft.rfft(y, L)
        sseq = np.fft.irfft(Y * Frev, L)[(n - 1):m]
        sseq = np.ascontiguousarray(sseq, dtype=np.float64)
        np.maximum(sseq, 0.0, out=sseq)

        ps = float(n) * sseq
        sma = float(np.max(ps))
        zx = -eta * (ps - sma) + 0.8 * logprior
        zx -= float(np.max(zx))
        zx = np.clip(zx, -50.0, 0.0)
        e = np.exp(zx)
        s = float(e.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
            x = prior.copy()
        else:
            x = e / s

    if count == 0:
        return x
    return _normalize_positive(0.5 * (avg / float(count)) + 0.5 * x)


def _mws_polish(p, L, m, n, time_limit, rng):
    p = _normalize_positive(p, 1e-12)
    c = _fft_self_conv(p, L, m)
    best_score = _score_c(c, n)
    best_p = p.copy()

    if time_limit <= 0.0:
        return best_p, best_score

    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + float(time_limit)
    inner = 14
    no_imp = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 0.3:
        prior = _random_prior(n, rng)
        x = _mws_direction(best_p, L, m, n, prior, inner, 2.0, 40.0)
        directions = [x]
        if n > 15:
            directions.append(_smooth_vector(x, 7))

        improved = False
        for d in directions:
            if improved:
                break
            for lam in (0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.35, 0.50, 0.70, 1.00):
                h = _normalize_positive((1.0 - lam) * best_p + lam * d, 1e-12)
                sc, _ = _score_p(h, L, m, n)
                if sc < best_score - 1e-13:
                    best_p = h
                    best_score = sc
                    improved = True
                    break

        if improved:
            no_imp = 0
            inner = min(inner + 1, 22)
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 5.0:
                break
        else:
            no_imp += 1
            if no_imp > 100:
                break

    return best_p, best_score


def _project_cap(c, cap):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    s = float(c.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        return c
    if cap * c.size < s:
        cap = s / float(c.size)
    if c.max() <= cap:
        return c / s

    work = c.copy()
    excess = float(np.sum(np.maximum(work - cap, 0.0)))
    free = work < cap

    for _ in range(100):
        if not np.any(free):
            break
        cnt = int(np.sum(free))
        if cnt == 0:
            break
        delta = excess / float(cnt)
        if np.max(work[free]) + delta <= cap:
            work[free] += delta
            break
        work[free] += delta
        over = free & (work >= cap)
        if np.any(over):
            over_amount = float(np.sum(work[over] - cap))
            work[over] = cap
            excess += over_amount
        free = work < cap

    work = np.maximum(work, 0.0)
    s2 = float(work.sum())
    if s2 > 0.0:
        work /= s2
    return work


def _fourier_projection(p, L, m, n, time_limit, rng):
    p = _normalize_positive(p, 1e-12)
    c = _fft_self_conv(p, L, m)
    best_score = _score_c(c, n)
    best_p = p.copy()

    if time_limit <= 0.0:
        return best_p, best_score

    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + float(time_limit)
    rate = 0.02
    blend = 0.5
    no_imp = 0
    hard_fail = 0

    for _ in range(100000):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 0.2:
            break

        c = _fft_self_conv(p, L, m)
        sc = _score_c(c, n)
        if sc < best_score - 1e-13:
            best_score = sc
            best_p = p.copy()
            no_imp = 0
            hard_fail = 0

        cap_c = (sc / (2.0 * float(n))) * (1.0 - rate)
        avg_c = 1.0 / float(m)
        cap_c = max(cap_c, avg_c * 1.02)

        if cap_c >= float(np.max(c)):
            rate *= 0.5
            if rate < 1e-5:
                break
            continue

        ct = _project_cap(c, cap_c)
        P = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
        delta = 1e-8 * max(float(c.mean()), 1e-30)
        ratio = (ct + delta) / (c + delta)
        ratio = np.clip(ratio, 0.05, 20.0)
        factor = np.sqrt(ratio)

        pnew = np.fft.irfft(P * factor, L)[:n]
        pnew = np.maximum(pnew, 0.0)
        snew = float(pnew.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(snew) or snew <= 0.0:
            pnew = best_p
        else:
            pnew /= snew

        accepted = False
        for alpha in (blend, 0.2, 1.0):
            ptr = _normalize_positive((1.0 - alpha) * p + alpha * pnew, 1e-12)
            cpt = _fft_self_conv(ptr, L, m)
            sct = _score_c(cpt, n)
            if sct < best_score - 1e-13 or sct < sc - 1e-12:
                p = ptr
                c = cpt
                sc = sct
                accepted = True
                if sct < best_score - 1e-13:
                    best_score = sct
                    best_p = p.copy()
                break

        if accepted:
            no_imp = 0
            hard_fail = 0
            rate = min(rate * 1.2, 0.20)
        else:
            no_imp += 1
            rate *= 0.7
            blend = min(blend * 1.2, 1.0)
            if no_imp > 80 or rate < 1e-5:
                prior = _random_prior(n, rng)
                p = _normalize_positive(0.8 * best_p + 0.2 * prior, 1e-12)
                c = _fft_self_conv(p, L, m)
                sc = _score_c(c, n)
                no_imp = 0
                rate = 0.02
                hard_fail += 1
                if hard_fail > 5:
                    break

    return best_p, best_score


def _sparsify_search(p, L, m, n):
    p = _normalize_positive(p)
    c = _fft_self_conv(p, L, m)
    cur_score = _score_c(c, n)
    best_p = p.copy()
    best_score = cur_score
    cur = p.copy()

    for frac in (0.01, 0.02, 0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.30):
        th = frac * float(np.mean(cur))
        q = cur.copy()
        q[q < th] = 0.0
        q = _normalize_positive(q)
        sc, _ = _score_p(q, L, m, n)
        if sc < cur_score - 1e-13:
            cur = q
            cur_score = sc
            if sc < best_score:
                best_p = q.copy()
                best_score = sc

    return best_p, best_score


def _internal_exact(seq):
    a = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if a.size == 0:
        return math.inf
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    n = int(a.size)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 0.01:
        return math.inf
    p = a / s
    L = 1 << (2 * n - 1).bit_length()
    m = 2 * n - 1
    c = _fft_self_conv(p, L, m)
    return float(2.0 * n * np.max(c))


def _exact_score(seq):
    try:
        val = evaluate_sequence(seq)
    except NameError:
        val = _internal_exact(seq)
    except Exception:
        val = _internal_exact(seq)
    if val is None:
        return math.inf
    return float(val)


def _run_search(n, time_limit, rng, global_arrays, global_scores, base=None):
    n = int(n)
    if n < 32 or time_limit <= 5.0:
        return None, math.inf

    L = 1 << (2 * n - 1).bit_length()
    m = 2 * n - 1
    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + float(time_limit)

    cands = _generate_candidates(n, rng, base)
    local_arrays = []
    local_scores = []

    best_arr = None
    best_score = math.inf

    for arr in cands:
        sc, _ = _score_p(arr, L, m, n)
        _add_candidate(local_arrays, local_scores, arr, sc, 40)
        if sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_arr = arr.copy()

    if best_arr is None:
        best_arr = _normalize_positive(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))
        best_score, _ = _score_p(best_arr, L, m, n)

    order = list(range(len(local_arrays)))
    order.sort(key=local_scores.__getitem__)

    quick_idx = []
    topk = min(4, len(order))
    quick_idx.extend(order[:topk])
    if len(order) > topk:
        rem_order = order[topk:]
        extra = int(min(2, rng.integers(1, len(rem_order) + 1)))
        picks = rng.choice(len(rem_order), size=extra, replace=False)
        for j in picks:
            quick_idx.append(rem_order[int(j)])
        uniq = []
        for i in quick_idx:
            if i not in uniq:
                uniq.append(i)
        quick_idx = uniq

    quick_total = min(0.25 * float(time_limit), 300.0) if time_limit > 300.0 else 0.20 * float(time_limit)
    per = max(10.0, quick_total / max(1, len(quick_idx)))

    for idx in quick_idx:
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 15.0:
            break
        tlim = min(per, rem)
        arr_opt, sc_opt = _local_optimize(local_arrays[idx], L, m, n, tlim, rng)
        _add_candidate(local_arrays, local_scores, arr_opt, sc_opt, 60)
        _add_candidate(global_arrays, global_scores, arr_opt, sc_opt, 80)
        if sc_opt < best_score:
            best_score = sc_opt
            best_arr = arr_opt.copy()

    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 30.0:
        tlim = min(rem * 0.45, 1000.0)
        if tlim > 20.0:
            arr_opt, sc_opt = _local_optimize(best_arr, L, m, n, tlim, rng)
            _add_candidate(global_arrays, global_scores, arr_opt, sc_opt, 80)
            if sc_opt < best_score:
                best_score = sc_opt
                best_arr = arr_opt.copy()

    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 40.0:
        tlim = min(rem * 0.30, 600.0)
        if tlim > 20.0:
            arr_opt, sc_opt = _mws_polish(best_arr, L, m, n, tlim, rng)
            _add_candidate(global_arrays, global_scores, arr_opt, sc_opt, 80)
            if sc_opt < best_score:
                best_score = sc_opt
                best_arr = arr_opt.copy()

    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 30.0:
        tlim = min(rem * 0.25, 400.0)
        if tlim > 15.0:
            arr_opt, sc_opt = _fourier_projection(best_arr, L, m, n, tlim, rng)
            _add_candidate(global_arrays, global_scores, arr_opt, sc_opt, 80)
            if sc_opt < best_score:
                best_score = sc_opt
                best_arr = arr_opt.copy()

    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 30.0:
        tlim = min(rem * 0.80, 600.0)
        if tlim > 15.0:
            arr_opt, sc_opt = _local_optimize(best_arr, L, m, n, tlim, rng)
            _add_candidate(global_arrays, global_scores, arr_opt, sc_opt, 80)
            if sc_opt < best_score:
                best_score = sc_opt
                best_arr = arr_opt.copy()

    arr_sp, sc_sp = _sparsify_search(best_arr, L, m, n)
    _add_candidate(global_arrays, global_scores, arr_sp, sc_sp, 80)
    if sc_sp < best_score:
        best_score = sc_sp
        best_arr = arr_sp.copy()

    _add_candidate(global_arrays, global_scores, best_arr, best_score, 80)
    return best_arr, best_score


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    start = time.monotonic()
    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 2400.0
    if not np.isfinite(budget) or budget <= 30.0:
        budget = 2400.0

    hard = start + max(10.0, float(budget) - 5.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    base = None
    try:
        base = height_sequence_1
    except NameError:
        base = None

    base_arr = None
    if base is not None:
        try:
            ba = np.asarray(base, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if ba.size > 0 and np.all(np.isfinite(ba)):
                base_arr = ba
        except Exception:
            base_arr = None

    global_arrays = []
    global_scores = []

    if base_arr is not None and base_arr.size > 256:
        primary_n = int(base_arr.size)
    else:
        primary_n = 8192
    primary_n = int(max(1024, min(primary_n, 10000)))

    total = max(120.0, hard - time.monotonic())
    if total > 900.0:
        t_primary = total * 0.62
        t_secondary = total * 0.28
    elif total > 400.0:
        t_primary = total * 0.85
        t_secondary = 0.0
    else:
        t_primary = total * 0.95
        t_secondary = 0.0

    p_prim, s_prim = _run_search(primary_n, t_primary, rng, global_arrays, global_scores, base=base_arr)
    if p_prim is not None:
        _add_candidate(global_arrays, global_scores, p_prim, s_prim, 80)
        print("primary_n=%d internal=%.9f elapsed=%.1f" % (primary_n, s_prim, time.monotonic() - start), flush=True)

    if t_secondary > 60.0:
        rem = max(60.0, hard - time.monotonic() - 30.0)
        t_sec = min(t_secondary, rem)
        if primary_n < 7500:
            sec_n = 8192
        else:
            sec_n = 6121
        p_sec, s_sec = _run_search(sec_n, t_sec, rng, global_arrays, global_scores, base=base_arr)
        if p_sec is not None:
            _add_candidate(global_arrays, global_scores, p_sec, s_sec, 80)
            print("secondary_n=%d internal=%.9f elapsed=%.1f" % (sec_n, s_sec, time.monotonic() - start), flush=True)

    best_exact = math.inf
    best_seq = None

    if global_scores:
        order = list(range(len(global_arrays)))
        order.sort(key=global_scores.__getitem__)
        tried = 0
        for idx in order:
            if time.monotonic() > hard - 10.0:
                break
            if tried >= 6:
                break
            arr = global_arrays[idx]
            seq = [float(v) for v in arr]
            val = _exact_score(seq)
            if np.isfinite(val) and val < best_exact:
                best_exact = val
                best_seq = seq
            tried += 1

    if best_seq is None:
        if global_scores:
            order = list(range(len(global_arrays)))
            order.sort(key=global_scores.__getitem__)
            arr = global_arrays[order[0]]
            best_seq = [float(v) for v in arr]
        else:
            best_seq = [1.0 / float(primary_n)] * primary_n

    return best_seq
```
